- **start**: Use when the user is unsure which buddy command to run, or starts a new task without specifying intent. Trigger phrases: "어디서부터 시작", "어떻게 시작해야 해", "buddy로 뭐 할까", "처음 써봐", "start over", "where to start", "begin a new project", "이 버그 좀", "이 프로젝트", "아이디어가 있어". Collects 3 inputs (project path / change type / commercial flag) via plain-language questions, then dispatches to concretize-idea (greenfield — no path) or assess-product-change (existing product — path provided). - **Start Database**: Start MongoDB via Docker ([A-Guy-educ/A-Guy](https://github.com/A-Guy-educ/A-Guy/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/db-start.md) · ★2)
- **Start Development**: Start the development server ([A-Guy-educ/A-Guy](https://github.com/A-Guy-educ/A-Guy/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/dev-start.md) · ★2)
- **Start Migration**: Begin a guided ERP migration. Discovers your current situation, chooses the right migration path, and creates a step-by-step plan to bring your data into Arfiti. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/blob/HEAD/arfiti-core-implementation-consultant/commands/start-migration.md) · ★5)
